Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 628 and the convergence rate is O(870/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_628(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 628. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^870(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.980 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: Let the chemistry structure S satisfy the conditions of existence and uniqueness framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 613. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 874 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 613 is immediate from the existence and uniqueness property. For the inductive step, we decompose the chemistry structure using the existence and uniqueness theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 874. Each subproblem has a solution by the existence and uniqueness fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
